1a:["$","$L27","1",{"children":["$","$L28",null,{"id":"lab-chain","html":"<div class=\"lab-head\"><h4>A nudge travels down the chain</h4><span class=\"tag\">Lab 6 · y = sin(x²)</span></div>\n<canvas aria-label=\"Three number lines x, u equals x squared, y equals sin u, showing how a small change is magnified\" class=\"tall\"></canvas>\n<div class=\"controls\">\n<label for=\"ch-x\">x <input id=\"ch-x\" max=\"2.2\" min=\"0.2\" step=\"0.01\" type=\"range\" value=\"1.1\"/></label>\n<label for=\"ch-dx\">Δx <input id=\"ch-dx\" max=\"0.3\" min=\"0.005\" step=\"0.005\" type=\"range\" value=\"0.15\"/></label>\n</div>\n<div class=\"readout\">\n<div>Δu / Δx ≈ du/dx = 2x<b class=\"c-f\" id=\"ch-1\">–</b></div>\n<div>Δy / Δu ≈ dy/du = cos u<b class=\"c-d\" id=\"ch-2\">–</b></div>\n<div>product of the two<b class=\"c-t\" id=\"ch-3\">–</b></div>\n<div>exact dy/dx = 2x·cos(x²)<b id=\"ch-4\">–</b></div>\n</div>\n<p class=\"note\">The inner function u = x² stretches the nudge by a factor of 2x. The outer function y = sin u stretches it again by cos u. Total stretch = the product. That is the chain rule.</p>"}]}]
1b:["$","$L27","2",{"children":["$","h3",null,{"id":"$undefined","children":"How to spot inside vs. outside"}]}]
